Compared to Searchlight, a representative energy-based method, RISE achieves 20.51x lower latency and 22.31% higher IoU. Compared to machine learning baselines, RISE improves IoU by 56.02% over DeepRadar while meeting the real-time deadline, which a GPU-accelerated U-Net exceeds by 213.38x.

</details>


### [118] [OrbitStream: Training-Free Adaptive 360-degree Video Streaming via Semantic Potential Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20999)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan,Zhangfei Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: OrbitStream是一个无需训练的360°视频流媒体框架，结合语义场景理解和鲁棒控制理论，用于远程操作中的自适应流媒体，在零训练情况下实现高视口预测准确率和QoE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决360°视频流媒体在远程操作中的双重挑战：不确定注视模式下的视口预测和波动无线信道上的码率自适应。现有数据驱动和深度强化学习方法虽然能实现高QoE，但其"黑盒"性质和对训练数据的依赖限制了在安全关键系统中的部署。

Method: 提出OrbitStream框架，结合语义场景理解和鲁棒控制理论。将视口预测建模为引力视口预测问题，语义对象产生吸引用户注视的势场。使用基于饱和的比例-微分控制器进行缓冲区调节。

Result: 在物体丰富的远程操作轨迹上，OrbitStream实现了94.7%的零样本视口预测准确率，接近轨迹外推基线(~98.5%)。在3600次蒙特卡洛模拟中，平均QoE为2.71，在12个评估算法中排名第二，接近最佳BOLA-E(2.80)，优于FastMPC(1.84)。系统平均决策延迟1.01ms，重缓冲事件最少。

Conclusion: OrbitStream通过提供具有可解释性和零训练开销的竞争性QoE，证明了基于物理的控制与语义建模相结合，为远程操作中的360°流媒体提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Adaptive 360° video streaming for teleoperation faces dual challenges: viewport prediction under uncertain gaze patterns and bitrate adaptation over volatile wireless channels. While data-driven and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) methods achieve high Quality of Experience (QoE), their "black-box" nature and reliance on training data can limit deployment in safety-critical systems. To address this, we propose OrbitStream, a training-free framework that combines semantic scene understanding with robust control theory. We formulate viewport prediction as a Gravitational Viewport Prediction (GVP) problem, where semantic objects generate potential fields that attract user gaze. Furthermore, we employ a Saturation-Based Proportional-Derivative (PD) Controller for buffer regulation. On object-rich teleoperation traces, OrbitStream achieves a 94.7\% zero-shot viewport prediction accuracy without user-specific profiling, approaching trajectory-extrapolation baselines ($\sim$98.5\%). Across 3,600 Monte Carlo simulations on diverse network traces, OrbitStream yields a mean QoE of 2.71. It ranks second among 12 evaluated algorithms, close to the top-performing BOLA-E (2.80) while outperforming FastMPC (1.84). The system exhibits an average decision latency of 1.01 ms with minimal rebuffering events. By providing competitive QoE with interpretability and zero training overhead, OrbitStream demonstrates that physics-based control, combined with semantic modeling, offers a practical solution for 360° streaming in teleoperation.

</details>


### [119] [Generative Artificial Intelligence Assisted Multi-modal Semantic Extraction for NOMA-based Image Transmissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21092)
*Songhan Zhao,Shimin Gong,Bo Gu,Hongyang Du,Xidong Mu,Zehui Xiong,Yuming Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出GAI辅助的语义通信框架，结合NOMA进行图像传输，通过联合优化语义特征选择和传输控制来最大化传输延迟与语义恢复精度的加权性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NOMA图像传输中，语义提取和传输控制共同影响语义恢复性能和传输开销，需要联合优化以提高系统性能

Method: 开发重要性感知和模型驱动的近端策略优化(IM-PPO)框架，量化并保留高重要性语义特征，同时使用基于模型的优化方法调整传输控制变量

Result: 数值结果表明，语义特征选择和传输控制的联合调整显著提高了语义恢复精度和传输延迟性能，IM-PPO框架相比基准方法有效利用了模型信息提高了学习效率

Conclusion: 提出的GAI辅助语义通信框架通过联合优化语义特征选择和传输控制，在NOMA图像传输中实现了更好的语义恢复性能和传输效率

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a generative artificial intelligence (GAI)-assisted semantic communication framework for non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA)-based image transmissions. Semantic users (SUs) extract cross-modal semantic features from the raw images, which are then used for image recovery by leveraging a GAI model. The GAI enhances the generalization and recovery of semantic image transmissions, while NOMA efficiently allocates transmission capacities to SUs based on their traffic demands. Thus, the semantic extraction and transmission control jointly affect both semantic recovery performance and transmission overhead. We maximize a weighted performance of transmission latency and semantic recovery accuracy by jointly optimizing the semantic feature selection at the semantic level, as well as the receive beamforming and NOMA decoding order at the transmission level. To reduce potential redundancy in semantic features and improve optimization efficiency, we develop an importance-aware and model-driven proximal policy optimization (IM-PPO) framework. Specifically, we quantify and retain high-importance semantic features to enhance the learning efficiency of PPO, while model-based optimization methods are used to adapt the transmission control variables. Numerical results validate that the joint adjustment of the semantic feature selection and the transmission control significantly improves the semantic recovery accuracy and the transmission latency performance. Moreover, the IM-PPO framework effectively leverages the model information to improve the learning efficiency compared to benchmark methods.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [The Deep-Match Framework for Event-Related Potential Detection in EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20258)
*Marek Zylinski,Bartosz Tomasz Smigielski,Gerard Cybulski*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种将ERP先验知识融入深度学习模型的Deep-MF方法，通过模板初始化卷积核来提升单试次ERP检测性能，在跨被试验证中获得了更好的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单试次ERP检测面临信噪比低的挑战，研究者希望探索将ERP模板先验知识融入深度学习模型是否能改善检测性能，特别是针对跨被试的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用Deep-Match框架进行ERP检测，使用两阶段训练：第一阶段训练编码器-解码器架构重构EEG信号学习紧凑表示；第二阶段替换解码器为检测模块并进行微调。评估了两个变体：标准随机初始化模型和用ERP模板初始化卷积核的Deep-MF模型。

Result: Deep-MF模型在大多数留一被试验证中略优于标准模型，平均F1分数更高（0.37 vs 0.34），表明对跨被试差异具有更好的鲁棒性。Deep-MF最佳F1分数达到0.71，超过标准模型的最佳分数0.59。

Conclusion: ERP信息化的卷积核初始化能在被试独立的单试次ERP检测中提供一致的改进，展示了将领域知识与深度学习架构结合用于EEG分析的潜力，为可穿戴EEG和被动脑机接口系统的实时认知监测迈出了一步。

Abstract: Reliable detection of event-related potentials (ERPs) at the single-trial level remains a major challenge due to the low signal-to-noise ratio EEG recordings. In this work, we investigate whether incorporating prior knowledge about ERP templates into deep learning models can improve detection performance. We employ the Deep-Match framework for ERP detection using multi-channel EEG signals. The model is trained in two stages. First, an encoder-decoder architecture is trained to reconstruct input EEG signals, enabling the network to learn compact signal representations. In the second stage, the decoder is replaced with a detection module, and the network is fine-tuned for ERP identification. Two model variants are evaluated: a standard model with randomly initialized filters and a Deep-MF model in which input kernels are initialized using ERP templates. Model performance is assessed on a single-trial ERP detection task using leave-one-subject-out validation. The proposed Deep-MF model slightly outperforms the detector with standard kernel initialization for most held-out subjects. Despite substantial inter-subject variability, Deep-MF achieves a higher average F1-score (0.37) compared to the standard network (0.34), indicating improved robustness to cross-subject differences. The best performance obtained by Deep-MF reaches an F1-score of 0.71, exceeding the maximum score achieved by the standard model (0.59). These results demonstrate that ERP-informed kernel initialization can provide consistent improvements in subject-independent single-trial ERP detection. Overall, the findings highlight the potential of integrating domain knowledge with deep learning architectures for EEG analysis. The proposed approach represents a step toward practical wearable EEG and passive brain-computer interface systems capable of real-time monitoring of cognitive processes.

</details>


### [121] [Cost-Aware Neural Early Stopping for Local Constraint OSD Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20385)
*Talha Akyildiz,Hessam Mahdavifar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出神经提前停止协议用于局部约束有序统计解码，通过单一权衡参数平衡误帧风险和搜索成本，显著减少平均测试错误模式数量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部约束有序统计解码在短码长线性码中提供强大的软判决性能，但其实际成本主要由测试错误模式数量决定，需要降低搜索复杂度

Method: 提出神经提前停止协议，使用FER对齐监督在预定义检查点训练模型，学习判断额外搜索是否可能改善当前最佳候选，通过比较预测的继续需求与TEP成本来决定停止

Result: 实验结果表明，该协议在多个码族中显著减少平均TEP数量，仅带来边际FER性能下降，且使用单一全局模型覆盖所有信噪比范围

Conclusion: 神经提前停止协议为LC-OSD提供了有效的成本控制机制，在保持解码性能的同时显著降低计算复杂度

Abstract: Local constraint ordered statistics decoding (LC-OSD) provides strong soft decision performance for short block length linear codes, but its practical cost is dominated by the number of tested error patterns (TEPs). This paper proposes a neural early stopping (NES) protocol for LC-OSD with explicit cost control through one trade-off parameter balancing frame error risk and search effort. The proposed approach is trained with frame error rate (FER)-aligned supervision at predefined checkpoints, and learns if additional search is still likely to improve the current best candidate. Later, stopping is decided by comparing predicted continuation need with a cost measured in TEPs. Experimental results across multiple code families show that the proposed protocol significantly reduces average TEP count with only marginal FER degradation, using a single global model for the range of all operating signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs).

</details>


### [122] [Sparse stability diagrams of LSCF method via strategic pole destabilization using orthogonal matching pursuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20614)
*Shogo Shimada,Akira Saito*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于正交匹配追踪(OMP)的模态参数识别方法，通过稀疏化特征多项式系数来消除稳定性图中的虚假极点，提高LSCF方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LSCF方法在提高多项式阶数时会产生非物理虚假极点，需要从稳定性图中手动消除，这降低了模态参数识别的效率和可靠性。

Method: 采用正交匹配追踪(OMP)算法，通过稀疏化特征多项式系数，策略性地使稳定但虚假的极点变得不稳定，从而在稳定性图中自动消除这些虚假极点。

Result: 方法在矩形板的数值仿真和实验数据（包括低阻尼和高阻尼情况）以及电动汽车电机定子芯的实际工业应用中均有效，能在不损失精度的情况下消除稳定性图中的虚假极点。

Conclusion: 提出的OMP稀疏化方法能够有效消除LSCF方法中的虚假极点，提高模态参数识别的鲁棒性和自动化程度，适用于从简单结构到复杂工业应用的多种场景。

Abstract: In various engineering fields including mechanical, aerospace, and civil engineering, the identification of modal parameters, including natural frequencies, damping ratios, and mode shapes, is crucial for determining the vibration characteristics of engineered structures. A common method for identifying the modal parameters of structures involves experimental modal analysis using frequency response functions (FRFs) obtained from forced vibration tests. The least squares complex frequency (LSCF) domain method is a widely-used frequency-domain curve-fitting method for the FRFs using the polynomials of high order, which can extract modal parameters with high accuracy. However, increasing the polynomial order tends to result in the generation of non-physical spurious poles that need to be eliminated from the stability diagrams. To overcome this issue, we propose a method that strategically destabilize the stable yet spurious poles of the characteristic polynomials by making their coefficients as sparse as possible, via orthogonal matching pursuit (OMP). This results in sparse stability diagrams because unstable poles can be eliminated from the diagrams. In this paper, the proposed method is first applied to a numerically-obtained FRFs of a rectangular plate using finite element model, and its validity is discussed. Then, the method is applied to experimentally-obtained FRFs of rectangular plates with low-damping and with high-damping. Furthermore, to confirm its applicability to industrial applications with realistic complexity, it has also been applied to the FRFs of the electric machine's stator core used for electric vehicles. Based on the results, we have confirmed that the spurious roots can be eliminated from the stability diagrams without compromising accuracy for the cases considered.

</details>


### [123] [A Channel Knowledge Map-Driven Two-Stage Coordinated User Scheduling in Multi-Cell Massive MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20621)
*Jiayang Wan,Hongwei Hou,Jiawei Zhuang,Wenjin Wang,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于多小区大规模MIMO系统的用户调度导向的信道知识图谱(US-CKM)及两阶段协调调度框架，通过利用位置信息与统计CSI的映射关系降低CSI获取开销，并引入可靠性引导机制增强动态散射环境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多小区大规模MIMO系统中的窄带协调用户调度面临计算复杂度和信令开销的挑战。传统方法需要频繁获取瞬时CSI，导致高开销；同时，动态散射环境下的不完美统计CSI会影响调度性能。

Method: 1. 开发用户调度导向的信道知识图谱(US-CKM)，建立位置信息与统计CSI的映射关系，实现快速SCSI检索和持久重用。2. 提出两阶段协调调度框架：第一阶段基于US-CKM进行小区内活跃用户选择，筛选具有良好信道增益和低小区内干扰的用户；第二阶段利用跨小区信道相关性进行小区间协调调度以抑制ICI。3. 引入可靠性引导机制，通过网格可靠性度量评估SCSI稳定性，对低可靠性网格实时获取瞬时CSI并与现有SCSI结合，增强动态环境下的鲁棒性。

Result: US-CKM能够显著降低CSI获取开销，通过SCSI的持久重用减少信令负担。两阶段调度框架在减少候选用户集的同时保持边际和速率损失最小，有效抑制了小区间干扰。可靠性引导机制确保了在动态散射环境下不完美SCSI条件下的鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的US-CKM和两阶段协调调度框架为多小区大规模MIMO系统提供了一种高效的用户调度解决方案，通过利用位置-SCSI映射降低开销，结合可靠性引导机制增强动态环境适应性，在计算复杂度和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates narrowband coordinated user scheduling in multi-cell massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. We formulate the problem under a spectral-efficiency maximization criterion, revealing inherent challenges in computational complexity and signaling overhead. To address these, we develop a user-scheduling-oriented CKM (US-CKM) and a US-CKM-driven two-stage coordinated scheduling framework. By exploiting the mapping between location information and statistical channel state information (SCSI), the system enables rapid SCSI retrieval and persistent reuse, substantially reducing CSI acquisition overhead. Embedding statistical channel correlation into the CKM further characterizes interuser interference patterns. The framework designs an intra-cell active-user selection scheme for the first stage and an inter-cell coordinated scheduling scheme for the second, both based on US-CKM entries. The first stage identifies users with favorable channel gains and low intra-cell interference, reducing the candidate set with marginal sum-rate loss. The second stage suppresses inter-cell interference (ICI) by exploiting cross-cell channel correlations. To enhance robustness against imperfect SCSI in dynamic scattering environments, we augment the framework with a reliability-guided mechanism. Instead of uniform treatment, we evaluate entry stability using a grid reliability metric quantifying channel measurement variance at sampling locations. Low-reliability grids are identified, and their instantaneous CSI is acquired in real time to integrate with existing SCSI. This process refines channel gain and spatial correlation characteristics, ensuring robust performance under imperfect conditions.

</details>


### [124] [The Binding Effect: Analyzing How Multi-Dimensional Cues Form Gender Bias in Instruction TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20743)
*Kuan-Yu Chen,Yi-Cheng Lin,Po-Chung Hsieh,Huang-Cheng Chou,Chih-Fan Hsu,Jeng-Lin Li,Hung-yi Lee,Jian-Jiun Ding*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究通过组合社会维度（社会地位、职业刻板印象、人物描述）分析ITTS中的性别偏见，发现多维度交互产生复杂偏见模式，这些偏见与预训练文本编码器的语义先验和训练数据分布相关，通用多样性提示无法消除这些根深蒂固的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前指令文本转语音（ITTS）的偏见评估通常依赖单变量测试，忽视了社会线索的组合结构。研究者旨在通过建模社会维度的组合来更全面地理解性别偏见，揭示被单变量基线方法忽略的复杂偏见模式。

Method: 将提示建模为社会地位、职业刻板印象和人物描述符的组合，分析开源ITTS模型。通过组合分析揭示社会维度之间的交互效应，并探究偏见与预训练文本编码器语义先验及训练数据分布的关系。

Result: 研究发现系统性的交互效应，社会维度相互调节产生复杂的偏见模式。这些偏见不仅限于表面特征，与预训练文本编码器的语义先验和训练数据的偏斜分布有强关联。通用多样性提示策略不足以覆盖这些根深蒂固的模式。

Conclusion: 需要组合分析方法来诊断生成语音中的潜在风险，单变量测试无法捕捉社会维度交互产生的复杂偏见模式。研究强调了理解偏见的多维度本质对于开发更公平的ITTS系统的重要性。

Abstract: Current bias evaluations in Instruction Text-to-Speech (ITTS) often rely on univariate testing, overlooking the compositional structure of social cues. In this work, we investigate gender bias by modeling prompts as combinations of Social Status, Career stereotypes, and Persona descriptors. Analyzing open-source ITTS models, we uncover systematic interaction effects where social dimensions modulate one another, creating complex bias patterns missed by univariate baselines. Crucially, our findings indicate that these biases extend beyond surface-level artifacts, demonstrating strong associations with the semantic priors of pre-trained text encoders and the skewed distributions inherent in training data. We further demonstrate that generic diversity prompting is insufficient to override these entrenched patterns, underscoring the need for compositional analysis to diagnose latent risks in generative speech.

</details>


### [125] [Enhanced Direction-Sensing Methods and Performance Analysis in Low-Altitude Wireless Network via a Rotation Antenna Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20784)
*Jinbing Jiang,Feng Shu,Minghao Chen,Jiatong Bai,Maolin Li,Yan Wang,Jiangzhou Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出PRI-IGSS方法，通过预旋转初始化和迭代贪婪空间谱搜索，解决可旋转阵列方向感知中计算复杂度高和时间效率低的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可旋转阵列感知方法（如RR-Root-MUSIC）存在多个机械旋转和重复特征分解操作，导致计算复杂度高、时间效率低。天线元件的方向性特性使得当发射源偏离阵列轴线时，接收信号功率会严重衰减，导致相应方向的感知性能下降。

Method: 提出三阶段低复杂度增强方向感知框架：1）将阵列法向量旋转到候选方向集，使用Root-MUSIC算法计算最大感知能量对应的DOA值；2）将阵列机械旋转到初始估计方向并保持固定；3）设计迭代贪婪空间谱搜索或接收波束成形方法，采用减少的搜索范围，并累加所有先前采样方差矩阵与当前矩阵以提供递增性能增益。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的PRI-IGSS方法性能远优于RR-Root-MUSIC，在均方误差方面达到CRLB（克拉美罗下界）。由于后者没有样本累积，PRI-IGSS在性能上具有显著优势。

Conclusion: PRI-IGSS方法通过预旋转初始化和迭代贪婪空间谱搜索，显著降低了计算复杂度，提高了时间效率，在方向感知性能上优于现有方法并达到理论下界。

Abstract: Due to the directive property of each antenna element, the received signal power can be severely attenuated when the emitter deviates from the array boresight, which will lead to a severe degradation in sensing performance along the corresponding direction. Although existing rotatable array sensing methods such as recursive rotation (RR-Root-MUSIC) can mitigate this issue by iteratively rotating and sensing, several mechanical rotations and repeated eigendecomposition operations are required to yield a high computational complexity and low time-efficiency. To address this problem, a pre-rotation initialization with recieve power as a rule is proposed to signifcantly reduce the computational complexity and improve the time-efficiency. Using this idea, a low-complexity enhanced direction-sensing framework with pre-rotation initialization and iterative greedy spatial-spectrum search (PRI-IGSS) is develped with three stages: (1) the normal vector of array is rotated to a set of candidates to find the opimal direction with the maximum sensing energy with the corresponding DOA value computed by the Root-MUSIC algorithm; (2) the array is mechanically rotated to the initial estimated direction and kept fixed; (3) an iterative greedy spatial-spectrum search or recieving beamforming method, moviated by reinforcement learning, is designed with a reduced search range and making a summation of all previous sampling variance matrices and the current one is adopted to provide an increasiong performance gain as the iteration process continues. To assess the performance of the proposed method, the corresponding CRLB is derived with a simplified rotation model. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed PRI-IGSS method performs much better than RR-Root-MUSIC and achieves the CRLB in term of mean squared error due to the fact there is no sample accumulation for the latter.

</details>


### [126] [Karhunen-Loève Expansion for Fluid Antenna Systems: Information-Theoretic Optimal Channel Compression and Outage Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20841)
*Tuo Wu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于Karhunen-Loève展开的流体天线系统信道分析框架，将N维相关信道分解为独立特征模，通过秩-K截断实现低维精确中断概率计算，避免现有块相关模型的乐观偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线系统通过密集端口切换实现空间分集，但空间相关的瑞利信道使精确中断分析难以处理。现有块相关模型对信道协方差矩阵施加结构近似，可能引入乐观性能偏差，需要更精确的分析框架。

Method: 提出基于Karhunen-Loève展开的框架，将N维相关FAS信道分解为独立特征模，进行可控的秩-K截断（K≪N），将中断分析简化为K维积分。推导了秩-1和秩-2情况的闭式中断表达式，并提供任意K的高斯-埃尔米特求积公式。

Result: 理论证明KL近似总是高估中断概率，提供保守保证；基于Slepian-Landau-Pollak定理确定仅需K*=2⌈W⌉+1个特征模（W为归一化孔径）；KL截断达到高斯率失真界，证明为信息论最优信道压缩。数值结果验证理论预测匹配蒙特卡洛仿真，KL框架在近似精度上优于BCM且避免乐观偏差。

Conclusion: KL展开框架为FAS信道分析提供了精确且保守的理论工具，有效自由度取决于孔径而非端口数，为安全系统设计提供理论保证，是信息论最优的信道压缩方法。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FAS) achieve spatial diversity by dynamically switching among $N$ densely packed ports, but the resulting spatially correlated Rayleigh channels render exact outage analysis intractable. Existing block-correlation models (BCM) impose structural approximations on the channel covariance matrix that can introduce optimistic performance bias. This paper proposes a principled Karhunen-Loève (KL) expansion framework that decomposes the $N$-dimensional correlated FAS channel into independent eigenmodes and performs a controlled rank-$K$ truncation, reducing the outage analysis to a $K$-dimensional integration with $K \ll N$. Closed-form outage expressions are derived for the rank-1 and rank-2 cases, and a general Gauss-Hermite quadrature formula is provided for arbitrary $K$. On the theoretical front, it is proved via Anderson's inequality that the KL approximation \emph{always} overestimates the outage probability, providing a conservative guarantee essential for secure system design. Leveraging the Slepian--Landau--Pollak concentration theorem, it is established that only $K^* = 2\lceil W \rceil + 1$ eigenmodes are needed regardless of $N$, where $W$ is the normalized aperture. It is further shown that the KL truncation achieves the Gaussian rate-distortion bound, certifying it as the information-theoretically optimal channel compression. Extensive numerical results confirm that (i) theoretical predictions match Monte Carlo simulations, (ii) the entropy fraction converges faster than the power fraction, (iii) the KL framework uniformly outperforms BCM in approximation accuracy while avoiding the optimistic bias inherent in block-diagonal models, and (iv) the effective degrees of freedom scale with the aperture rather than the number of ports.

</details>


### [127] [A Gaussian Process Framework for Outage Analysis in Continuous-Aperture Fluid Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20846)
*Tuo Wu,Jianchao Zheng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文为流体天线系统通信的中断概率建立了全面的分析框架，通过将天线建模为连续孔径并用高斯核近似Jakes空间相关性，提出了三种互补的分析策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线系统（FAS）通过天线位置调整实现空间分集，但现有分析多基于离散端口和块相关模型，存在乐观偏差。需要开发连续孔径模型和精确分析框架来准确评估FAS中断性能。

Method: 1. 推导高斯核下的Karhunen-Loève展开，获得秩-1和秩-2截断的闭式中断表达式，以及任意秩K的Gauss-Hermite公式；2. 通过Slepian不等式和高斯比较定理建立严格的双侧中断界；3. 使用Adler-Taylor期望欧拉特征方法和Piterbarg定理发展连续孔径极值理论。

Result: 高斯近似在W≤2时相对中断误差小于10%；连续孔径公式仅需N≈10W个端口即可收敛；中断概率表达式P_out≈1-e^{-x}(1+π√2Wx)仅依赖于孔径W和阈值x，与端口数N无关；Jakes和高斯模型具有相同结果；推导了深中断区域的Pickands常数修正和阈值相关的有效分集度N_eff≈1+π√2Wx。

Conclusion: 该框架为FAS中断分析提供了精确、实用的工具，高斯核近似在合理孔径范围内具有良好精度，连续孔径模型揭示了FAS性能的基本极限，为系统设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper develops a comprehensive analytical framework for the outage probability of fluid antenna system (FAS)-aided communications by modeling the antenna as a continuous aperture and approximating the Jakes (Bessel) spatial correlation with a Gaussian kernel $ρ_G(δ) = e^{-π^2δ^2}$. Three complementary analytical strategies are pursued. First, the Karhunen--Loève (KL) expansion under the Gaussian kernel is derived, yielding closed-form outage expressions for the rank-1 and rank-2 truncations and a Gauss--Hermite formula for arbitrary rank~$K$, with effective degrees of freedom $K_{\mathrm{eff}}^G \approx π\sqrt{2}\, W$. Second, rigorous two-sided outage bounds are established via Slepian's inequality and the Gaussian comparison theorem: by sandwiching the true correlation between equi-correlated models with $ρ_{\min}$ and $ρ_{\max}$, closed-form upper and lower bounds that avoid the optimistic bias of block-correlation models are obtained. Third, a continuous-aperture extreme value theory is developed using the Adler--Taylor expected Euler characteristic method and Piterbarg's theorem. The resulting outage expression $P_{\mathrm{out}} \approx 1 - e^{-x}(1 + π\sqrt{2}\, W\, x)$ depends only on the aperture~$W$ and threshold~$x$, is independent of the port count~$N$, and is identical for the Jakes and Gaussian models since both share the second spectral moment $λ_2 = 2π^2$. A Pickands-constant refinement for the deep-outage regime and a threshold-dependent effective diversity $N_{\mathrm{eff}} \approx 1 + π\sqrt{2}\, W\, x$ are further derived. Numerical results confirm that the Gaussian approximation incurs less than 10\% relative outage error for $W \leq 2$ and that the continuous-aperture formula converges with as few as $N \approx 10W$ ports.

</details>


### [128] [Terahertz Beamforming and Group Sparse Channel Estimation Relying on Low-Resolution ADCs in MU Hybrid MIMO systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20878)
*Abhisha Garg,Suraj Srivastava,Akash Kumar,Nimish Yadav,Aditya Jagannatham,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于贝叶斯学习的统一波束成形与信道估计框架，针对太赫兹频段低分辨率ADC和频率相关传播效应，采用双宽带信道模型和RRC脉冲成形，通过Bussgang分解处理非线性失真，利用多用户共享稀疏性提出HBG-SR信道学习技术，结合TTD补偿波束倾斜效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹频段通信面临低分辨率ADC带来的非线性失真和频率相关传播效应挑战，传统方法难以有效处理宽带系统中的波束倾斜效应和信道估计问题，需要开发能够同时处理这些挑战的统一框架。

Method: 1) 建立包含RRC脉冲成形的双宽带信道模型；2) 使用Bussgang分解将低分辨率ADC非线性失真线性化；3) 提出基于层次贝叶斯群稀疏回归(HBG-SR)的信道学习技术，利用多用户场景中的共享稀疏性；4) 利用估计的AoA/AoD主导指数配置TTD元件，实现跨子载波的精确波束对齐和波束倾斜效应补偿。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的信道估计器和TTD辅助波束成形架构在实用宽带太赫兹系统约束下具有高效性、鲁棒性和性能增益，能够有效补偿波束倾斜效应并实现精确波束对齐。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个统一的贝叶斯学习框架，成功解决了太赫兹宽带系统中低分辨率ADC非线性失真、频率相关传播效应和波束倾斜效应的综合挑战，通过HBG-SR信道学习和TTD配置实现了高效的信道估计和波束成形。

Abstract: A unified beamforming and channel estimation framework relying on Bayesian learning is conceived. Recognizing the limitations imposed by low-resolution analog-to-digital converter (ADCs) and frequency-dependent propagation effects occurring in the Terahertz (THz) band, we formulate a dual-wideband channel model incorporating root raised cosine (RRC) pulse shaping. To address the non-linear distortions introduced by low-resolution ADCs, Bussgang decomposition is employed, leading to a tractable linearized inference process. By leveraging the shared sparsity inherent in a multi-user (MU) scenario of THz systems, we propose a Hierarchical Bayesian Group-sparse Regression (HBG-SR) based channel learning technique that exploits the group-sparse structure of THz band channels. The estimated dominant angle-of-arrival/ angle-of-departure (AoA/AoD) indices are then exploited for appropriately configuring the true-time-delay (TTD) elements in the hybrid transceiver, enabling precise beam alignment across subcarriers and the effective compensation of the beam-squint effect occurring in wideband THz systems. Extensive simulation results validate the efficiency of the proposed channel estimator and the TTD-aided beamforming architecture, highlighting their robustness and performance gains under practical wideband THz system constraints.

</details>


### [129] [Exploiting Self-Sustainable Information-Bearing RIS in Underlay CR-NOMA Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21087)
*Zeyang Sun,Shuai Han,Chenyu Wu,Sai Xu,Yuanwei Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了自可持续信息承载可重构智能表面辅助的非正交多址认知无线电系统，通过联合优化波束成形、反射系数和功率分配比来最大化加权和频谱效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RIS仅能被动反射信号，而信息承载RIS(IB-RIS)能够在反射信号中嵌入信息，实现同时的能量收集和信息传输。将这种自可持续的IB-RIS(SIB-RIS)与NOMA和认知无线电结合，可以构建更高效、绿色的通信系统。

Method: 提出SIB-RIS辅助的NOMA认知无线电系统模型，其中多天线主发射器同时服务主用户并照射次级节点。SIB-RIS在每个反射单元同时进行能量收集和基于反向散射的信息嵌入。采用块坐标下降优化框架，结合分数规划、拉格朗日对偶、二次变换和凸差规划方法，联合优化主发射器波束成形向量、SIB-RIS反射系数和功率分配比。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的SIB-RIS辅助NOMA认知无线电系统在加权和频谱效率方面显著优于正交多址和主动天线方案。2比特离散相位SIB-RIS实现能够达到接近连续相位的性能，验证了低分辨率架构的实用性。

Conclusion: SIB-RIS辅助的NOMA认知无线电系统通过同时实现能量收集和信息嵌入，为自可持续绿色通信提供了有效解决方案。所提出的优化算法能够有效解决复杂的非凸问题，离散相位实现方案保证了系统的实际可行性。

Abstract: Information-bearing reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (IB-RIS) provide a promising solution to self-sustainable and green communications by harvesting ambient radio frequency energy while embedding information via passive reflection. This paper investigates a self-sustainable IB-RIS (SIB-RIS)-assisted non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) network operating in an underlay cognitive radio (CR) system. Specifically, a multi-antenna primary transmitter (PT) serves a primary user (PU) and concurrently illuminates the secondary nodes, which enables each SIB-RIS to perform simultaneous energy harvesting and backscatter-based information embedding at each RIS. Based on this model, a weighted sum spectral efficiency (WSSE) maximization problem is formulated for the secondary network by jointly optimizing the PT transmit beamforming vector, the SIB-RIS reflection coefficients, and the power-splitting ratios. To tackle the intricately-coupled non-convex problem, an efficient block coordinate descent (BCD) optimization framework is developed, which leverages fractional programming via Lagrangian dual and quadratic transforms together with a difference-of-convex programming approach. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed SIB-RIS-assisted NOMA CR system yields substantial WSSE gains over both orthogonal multiple access (OMA)-based and active antenna schemes. Moreover, a 2-bit discrete-phase SIB-RIS implementation achieves competitive to which WSSE performance, confirming the practicality of the low-resolution architecture.

</details>


### [130] [On the Robustness of AoA as an Authentication Feature Under Spoofing: Fundamental Limits from Misspecified Cramer Rao Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21219)
*Sotiris Skaperas,Arsenia Chorti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究AoA作为物理层认证特征在欺骗攻击下的鲁棒性，分析模型失配情况下的性能界限和检测概率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究角度到达(AoA)作为物理层认证特征的鲁棒性，特别是在欺骗攻击场景下，当攻击者使用多天线从不同角度发送信号时，验证器仍假设单源模型导致的模型失配问题

Method: 假设数字均匀线性阵列验证器，推导模型失配下的Cramer-Rao界闭式表达式、认证决策阈值、欺骗检测概率、虚警概率和漏检概率

Result: 仿真结果验证了理论推导，并揭示了信噪比、阵列几何结构、欺骗预编码和快照数量对认证鲁棒性的影响

Conclusion: AoA作为物理层认证特征在欺骗攻击下存在模型失配问题，通过理论分析和仿真验证了性能界限，为评估认证系统鲁棒性提供了理论框架

Abstract: The robustness of angle of arrival (AoA) as a physical layer authentication (PLA) feature under spoofing attacks is studied, assuming a digital uniform linear array verifier. The verifier estimates the AoA assuming a legitimate user's single source model, whereas the received signal is generated by a multi antenna adversary at a different angle, leading to a model mismatch. Closed form expressions are derived for the misspecified Cramer Rao bound, the PLA decision threshold, the spoofing detection, false alarm and misdetection probabilities. Simulation results validate the theoretical findings and highlight the impact of the signal to noise ratio, array geometries, spoofing precoding and number of snapshots on authentication robustness.

</details>


### [131] [Explore the Capacity of Near Field Channel using Gaussian Beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21339)
*Chenxi Zhu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在Hermite-Gaussian波束域中近似近场MIMO信道的方法，使用HG模式来近似自由空间LOS环境下的信道，通过迭代扩展波束空间维度来逼近最大信道容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近场MIMO系统中获取详细原生信道信息通常困难且昂贵，而信道容量计算又需要详细的信道信息。因此需要开发一种能够减少信道估计开销的方法来近似信道并计算容量。

Method: 使用Hermite-Gaussian模式来近似自由空间LOS环境下方形天线阵列之间的信道。HG模式能够有效表示原生信道的主导奇异模式，在HG波束空间中实现准确的信道估计和容量计算。提出了一种迭代算法，通过逐步扩展波束空间维度来逼近最大信道容量。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法收敛速度快，能够显著减少信道估计开销。HG模式能够高效表示信道的主导奇异模式，在波束空间中实现准确的信道容量计算。

Conclusion: 提出的HG波束域近似方法为近场MIMO系统提供了一种有效的信道表示和容量计算方案，通过减少信道估计开销，在实际系统中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Channel capacity lies at the core of wireless communication, yet determining it typically requires detailed channel information between the transmitter and receiver. For near field MIMO systems, obtaining the detailed native channel is often difficult or expensive. This paper develops a scheme to approximate the near field channel in a Gaussian beam domain. Hermite Gaussian (HG) modes are used to approximate the channel between a pair of square antenna arrays in a free space line of sight (LOS) environment. We show that HG modes efficiently represent the dominant singular modes of the native channel, enabling accurate channel estimation and capacity computation in the HG beam space. An iterative algorithm is proposed to approach the maximal channel capacity by gradually expanding the beam space dimension. Simulation results demonstrate that the method converges rapidly and significantly reduces channel estimation overhead.

</details>


### [132] [Homodyne vs. Heterodyne Architectures in Sub-THz Transceivers: A Phase Noise Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21384)
*Didem Aydoğan,Korkut Kaan Tokgöz*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 比较同相和异相架构在亚太赫兹OFDM系统中的相位噪声影响，异相架构通过低频振荡器分布频率转换降低相位噪声方差，在更高频段提供更好鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究振荡器相位噪声对亚太赫兹OFDM收发器架构的影响，特别是同相和异相设计的比较，为未来亚太赫兹无线系统设计提供架构层面的优化方案。

Method: 采用Hexa-X兼容的相位噪声模型进行理论分析，证明异相架构通过低频振荡器分布频率转换降低总累积相位噪声方差；在70 GHz和140 GHz频段进行OFDM仿真验证。

Result: 异相架构将主要损伤从载波间干扰转变为公共相位误差，在毫米波频段同相架构仍具竞争力，但在更高亚太赫兹载波频率下，异相设计对相位噪声具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 收发器架构是未来亚太赫兹无线系统中放宽振荡器和锁相环约束的关键设计杠杆，异相架构在更高频段能有效缓解相位噪声问题。

Abstract: This letter examines the impact of oscillator phase noise on sub-terahertz OFDM transceiver architectures, with a focus on the comparison between homodyne and heterodyne designs. Using a Hexa-X compliant phase noise model, we analytically show that heterodyne architectures reduce the total accumulated phase noise variance by distributing frequency translation across lower-frequency oscillators under realistic phase-noise scaling laws, thereby shifting the dominant impairment from inter-carrier interference to common phase error. OFDM simulations at 70 GHz and 140 GHz demonstrate that while homodyne architectures remain competitive at mmWave frequencies, heterodyne designs provide improved robustness to phase noise at higher sub-THz carriers. These results highlight transceiver architecture as a key design lever for relaxing oscillator and phase-locked loop constraints in future sub-THz wireless systems.

</details>


### [133] [CGFormer: A Cross-Attention Based Grid-Free Transformer for Radio Map Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21408)
*Haihan Nan,Emmanuel Obeng Frimpong,Zhi Tian,Yue Wang,Lingjia Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于交叉注意力网格自由Transformer的无线地图估计方法，通过轻量级空间嵌入模块和环境知识融合，实现高效灵活的信号预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线地图估计方法存在显著局限性：基于网格的方法在追求精度时导致计算效率低下且缺乏离网格预测灵活性；网格自由方法虽然能实现任意位置预测，但需要大量预处理开销构建空间表示，导致高复杂度和适应性受限。

Method: 提出交叉注意力网格自由Transformer模型：1) 轻量级空间嵌入模块，将环境知识融入高维特征构建；2) 交叉注意力Transformer，建模目标点与测量点之间的空间相关性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法将RMSE降低高达6%，优于基于网格和网格自由的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的交叉注意力网格自由Transformer模型有效解决了现有无线地图估计方法的计算效率、灵活性和适应性限制，通过创新的空间嵌入和注意力机制实现了更优的性能。

Abstract: Radio map estimation (RME), which predicts wireless signal metrics at unmeasured locations from sparse measurements, has attracted growing attention as a key enabler of intelligent wireless networks. The majority of existing RME techniques employ grid-based strategies to process sparse measurements, where the pursuit of accuracy results in significant computational inefficiency and inflexibility for off-grid prediction. In contrast, grid-free approaches directly exploit coordinate features to capture location-specific spatial dependencies, enabling signal prediction at arbitrary locations without relying on predefined grids. However, current grid-free approaches demand substantial preprocessing overhead for constructing the spatial representation, leading to high complexity and constrained adaptability. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel cross-attention grid-free based transformer model for RME. We introduce a lightweight spatial embedding module that incorporates environmental knowledge into high-dimensional feature construction. A cross-attention transformer then models the spatial correlation between target and measurement points. The simulation results demonstrate that our proposed method reduces RMSE by up to 6%, outperforming grid-based and gridfree baselines.

</details>


### [134] [Site-Specific Channel Modeling and Optimization of RIS-Assisted Multiuser MISO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21433)
*Ziqi Liu,Wei Yu,Sean Victor Hum*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于物理的信道建模与优化框架，用于特定场景下RIS辅助的下行多用户MISO通信系统，采用混合射线追踪和全波电磁分析方法构建确定性信道模型，并通过交替优化方案联合更新基站波束成形器和RIS负载阻抗以最大化最小可达速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在特定场景环境中，需要可靠地评估可重构智能表面(RIS)部署对系统级性能的影响。现有模型往往忽略实际传播效应如多径传播、RIS散射行为和互耦效应，因此需要开发一个能够显式捕捉这些物理效应的确定性信道建模框架，为RIS辅助的MU-MISO系统设计和部署提供实用指导。

Method: 1. 开发混合射线追踪(RT)和全波电磁分析方法，构建确定性信道模型，通过非对角负载阻抗表示显式捕捉多径传播、RIS散射行为和互耦效应。2. 基于该模型，提出交替优化方案，在总发射功率约束和实际电容限制下，联合更新基站波束成形器和RIS负载阻抗以最大化最小可达速率。3. 采用列配对1比特控制模式操作RIS，实现对所有可实现配置的穷举评估。4. 在仿真和测量中通过可达速率直方图在分布层面比较性能，并考察小范围用户位置变化下的表现。

Result: 仿真与测量结果显示出良好的一致性，表明所提出的框架能够可靠地捕捉实际性能趋势。该框架为特定场景环境中RIS辅助的MU-MISO系统设计和部署提供了有用指导，特别是在评估RIS部署对系统级性能影响方面表现出可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于物理的信道建模与优化框架能够可靠地评估特定场景环境中RIS辅助的MU-MISO系统性能。通过混合射线追踪和全波电磁分析方法构建的确定性信道模型，以及交替优化方案，该框架为RIS部署的系统级影响提供了可靠的初始评估，并为实际系统设计和部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: This paper presents a physics-based channel modeling and optimization framework for reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted downlink multi-user multiple-input single-output (MU-MISO) communication systems in site-specific environments. A hybrid ray-tracing (RT) and full-wave electromagnetic analysis approach is developed to construct a deterministic channel model that explicitly captures multipath propagation, RIS scattering behavior, and mutual coupling effects through a non-diagonal load impedance representation. Based on this model, an alternating optimization scheme jointly updates the base-station (BS) beamformer and RIS load impedances to maximize the minimum achievable rate under a total transmit power constraint and practical capacitance limits. The objective of the proposed framework is to provide a reliable initial assessment of the system-level impact of RIS deployment in realistic propagation scenarios. To evaluate this capability, the RIS is operated in a column-paired 1-bit control mode that enables exhaustive evaluation of all realizable configurations in both simulation and measurement. Performance is compared at the distribution level through achievable-rate histograms across all configurations and further examined under small user-location variations. The observed agreement between simulation and measurement demonstrates that the proposed framework reliably captures practical performance trends and provides useful guidance for the design and deployment of RIS-assisted MU-MISO systems in site-specific environments.

</details>


### [135] [Rydberg Atomic Receivers for Net-Zero 6G Wireless Communication and Sensing: Progress, Experiments, and Sustainable Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21498)
*Yi Tao,Zhen Gao,Zhiao Zhu,De Mi,Zhonghuai Wu,Zijian Zhang,Fusang Zhang,Dezhi Zheng,Sheng Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文系统阐述了里德堡原子接收器在量子无线通信和传感中的基本原理、最新进展及创新实验，首次实验验证了基于RARE的正交频分复用传输，并探讨了深度学习优化量子无线系统的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全球推动信息通信技术产业绿色转型和利用技术创新实现净零碳目标的背景下，里德堡原子接收器因其在微型天线设计、高灵敏度、强抗干扰性和紧凑尺寸方面的显著优势，成为满足零碳通信需求的竞争性替代方案。

Method: 系统阐述RARE的基本原理、最新进展和创新实验；首次实验验证基于RARE的正交频分复用传输；探索深度学习在优化量子无线系统设计中的应用潜力。

Result: 成功实验验证了基于里德堡原子接收器的正交频分复用传输，揭示了深度学习设计在优化量子无线系统中的潜力，为基于里德堡的无线系统发展指明了关键路径。

Conclusion: 里德堡原子接收器在量子无线通信和传感中展现出巨大潜力，其与现有前沿应用场景的整合前景广阔，为开发基于里德堡的无线系统提供了重要技术路径。

Abstract: Against the backdrop of the global drive to advance the green transformation of the information and communications technology (ICT) industry and leverage technological innovation to facilitate the achievement of Net-Zero carbon goals, research into Rydberg atomic receivers (RAREs) is gaining significant interest. RAREs leverage the electron transition phenomenon for signal reception, offering significant advantages over conventional radio frequency receivers in terms of miniaturized antenna design, high sensitivity, robust interference resistance, and compact form factors, which positions them as a competitive alternative for meeting zero-carbon communication demands. This article systematically elaborates on the basic principle, state-of-the-art progress, and novel experiments of RAREs in quantum wireless communication and sensing. In this first-of-its-kind work, we experimentally verify the RARE-based orthogonal frequency division multiplexing transmission and reveal the potential of deep learning design in optimizing quantum wireless systems. Finally, we delve into the prospect of integrating RARE with existing cutting-edge application scenarios, while mapping out critical pathways for developing Rydberg-based wireless systems.

</details>


### [136] [Digital Self-Interference Cancellation in Full-Duplex Radios: A Fundamental Limit Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21561)
*Limin Liao,Jun Sun,Junzhi Wang,Chao Deng,Jizhao Wang,Fangsen Li,Yingzhuang Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文通过广义拉盖尔多项式正交展开分析并行Hammerstein数字自干扰消除器的性能极限，提出优化导频序列准则以减少重构误差，仿真显示优化导频比传统HE-LTF提升超10dB增益


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字自干扰消除在带内全双工无线电中至关重要，但其性能极限尚不明确。本文旨在通过分析实际最常用的并行Hammerstein消除器来探索数字自干扰消除的基本性能极限。

Method: 1. 使用广义拉盖尔多项式对并行Hammerstein消除器进行正交展开，简化分析过程；2. 分析最小二乘估计器下残余自干扰功率，考虑截断误差、重构误差和发射机噪声；3. 提出优化导频序列准则，最小化格拉姆矩阵的香农秩与最小特征值的乘积，以减少重构误差。

Result: 1. 证明当导频序列服从高斯分布时，广义拉盖尔多项式消除器权重的LS估计器是渐近无偏的；2. 仿真结果显示，使用优化的单OFDM符号导频序列，相比传统HE-LTF导频序列，并行Hammerstein消除器可获得超过10dB的增益，残余自干扰功率可低至-87.6dBm。

Conclusion: 通过广义拉盖尔多项式正交展开方法，本文阐明了并行Hammerstein数字自干扰消除器的性能极限，提出的导频序列优化准则能显著降低重构误差，为实现高效数字自干扰消除提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Digital self-interference cancellation (D-SIC) plays a crucial role in in-band full-duplex radios. Unfortunately, its fundamental limit remains unclear. In this paper, we aim to address this problem by exploring the performance limit of the parallel Hammerstein (PH) canceller for D-SIC, which is most commonly used in practice. First, a comprehensive analysis of the power of the residual self-interference (RSI) after the PH canceller with the least squares (LS) estimator is provided, which takes into account the truncation error, reconstruction error and transmitter noise. Specifically, the analysis is greatly simplified by equivalently expanding the PH canceller via generalized Laguerre polynomials (GLP), which enjoys the desirable property of mutual orthogonality among the basis functions. As a by-product of this orthogonal expansion, we establish that the LS estimator for the weights of the GLP canceller is asymptotically \textit{unbiased}, if the pilot sequence is Gaussian distributed. Second, in order to minimize the reconstruction error of the PH canceller, we propose a succinct criterion for optimizing the pilot sequence, which essentially seeks for small eigenvalue spread and large minimum eigenvalue of the Gram matrix corresponding to the pilot sequence. Specifically, the criterion is to minimize the product of the Shannon rank, an effective rank of a positive semidefinite matrix and the minimum eigenvalue of the Gram matrix. Simulation results demonstrate that with the optimized pilot sequence of a single OFDM symbol, over 10 dB gain can be achieved compared to the conventional pilot sequence (HE-LTF) for the PH canceller, and the corresponding RSI can be as low as -87.6 dBm.

</details>


### [137] [Battery health reporting fails independent validation across manufacturers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21592)
*Jeongju Park,Kyungkak Kim,Seungho Geum,Junhyung Lee,Hyeongyu Son,Sekyung Han*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究发现电动汽车电池健康状态（SOH）报告存在系统性不可靠问题，制造商之间的BMS数据与实际容量差异显著，提出了一种不依赖BMS的独立电化学标记方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证电动汽车电池管理系统（BMS）报告的电池健康状态（SOH）的可靠性，因为这是车主和监管机构获取电池健康状况的主要指标，但此前缺乏跨制造商的独立验证研究。

Method: 采用流行病学研究方法，分析了1,114辆来自5家制造商的电动汽车，历时375天。同时开发了不依赖BMS数据的制造商独立电化学标记方法，并在实验室使用与车队相同的电池单元进行验证，测试了60个电压窗口的部分电压窗口充电测量。

Result: 研究发现：1）每个车型内部存在12-25%的实际容量差异；2）BMS SOH无法有效跟踪这些差异，相关性从ρ=0.10（不显著）到ρ=0.62（仅限严格过滤条件）；3）384辆车完全不提供SOH数据；4）独立电化学标记方法在所有平台上实现74-89%的退化分类准确率；5）实验室验证显示部分电压窗口充电测量与参考容量相关性ρ>0.80（所有60个电压窗口，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 电池健康报告存在结构性信息不对称问题，这对欧盟电池法规的2027年SOH透明度要求、加州先进清洁汽车II耐久性要求、保修执行、二手车估值、维修权立法和二次利用电池市场具有直接影响。

Abstract: Battery state-of-health (SOH) reported by on-board battery management systems (BMS) is the primary metric available to electric vehicle (EV) owners and regulators, yet no study has validated its reliability across manufacturers against independent measurements. Here we show, through an epidemiological study of 1,114 EVs spanning five manufacturers and 375 days, that battery health reporting is fundamentally unreliable: real capacity differences of 12-25% exist within every model, but BMS SOH fails to track them, with correlations ranging from \r{ho} = 0.10 (non-significant) to \r{ho} = 0.62 only under restrictive filtering, while 384 vehicles do not expose SOH at all. A manufacturer-independent electrochemical marker achieves 74-89% degradation classification accuracy across all platforms without requiring BMS data, and a controlled laboratory validation on cells identical to those in the fleet confirms that partial-voltage-window charge measurements track reference capacity with \r{ho} > 0.80 across all 60 voltage windows (p < 0.001). These findings reveal a structural information asymmetry with direct implications for the EU Battery Regulation's 2027 SOH transparency mandate, California's Advanced Clean Cars (ACC) II durability requirements, warranty enforcement, used-vehicle valuation, right-to-repair legislation, and second-life battery markets.

</details>


### [138] [Extreme-MIMO Field Trials in 7 GHz Band: Unlocking the Potential of New Spectrum for 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21632)
*Seunghyun Lee,Jungmin Yoon,Sangwon Jung,Young-Han Nam,Gary Xu,Chan-Byoung Chae,Juho Lee,Jianzhong,Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向6G的256端口7GHz X-MIMO系统，通过系统级仿真和原型验证，展示了在7GHz频段实现8层单用户MIMO和超过3Gbps吞吐量的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 7GHz频段作为6G有前景的上中频段，需要在覆盖和容量间取得平衡。现有5G的64端口部署无法满足未来需求，需要更强的波束赋形和空间复用能力。

Method: 1) 通过系统级仿真分析基站和用户设备数字天线端口数量增加带来的吞吐量收益和设计权衡；2) 开发256端口7GHz基站和用户设备原型；3) 在城市室外环境进行现场试验测量；4) 基于测量数据进行信道分析。

Result: 1) 展示了128端口和256端口配置的比较；2) 在100MHz带宽上实现了8层下行单用户MIMO，在城市室外传播条件下单个用户吞吐量超过3Gbps；3) 信道分析表明X-MIMO的大数字孔径即使在有有限主导角度簇的情况下也能支持高阶空间复用。

Conclusion: 7GHz X-MIMO系统是6G的实用架构，现场试验验证了其可行性。文章还指出了实现7GHz X-MIMO系统实际部署的关键挑战和研究方向。

Abstract: The frequency range around 7 GHz has emerged as a promising upper mid-band spectrum for 6th generation (6G), offering a practical balance between coverage and capacity. To fully exploit this band, however, future systems require substantially stronger beamforming and spatial multiplexing capability than today's 5G 64-port commercial deployments. This article investigates extreme multiple-input multiple-output (X-MIMO) with 256 digital ports as a practical 6G architecture for 7 GHz operation. First, through system-level simulations, we examine the throughput benefits and design trade-offs of increasing the number of base station (BS) and user equipment (UE) digital antenna ports, including comparisons between 128-port and 256-port configurations. We then present a 256-port 7 GHz BS and UE prototype and report field-trial results obtained in urban outdoor environments. The measurements demonstrate the feasibility of 8-layer downlink single-user MIMO over a 100 MHz bandwidth, achieving more than 3 Gbps for a single user under urban outdoor propagation conditions. Channel analysis based on measured data further suggests how the large digital aperture of X-MIMO supports high-order spatial multiplexing even with limited dominant angular clusters. Finally, we identify key challenges and outline research directions toward practical deployment of 7 GHz X-MIMO systems for 6G.

</details>


### [139] [Near-Field Wideband Channel Estimation for Extremely Large-Scale RIS-Aided Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21709)
*Lanqing Zhi,Hongwei Wang,Lingxiang Li,Zhi Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种针对OFDM系统中XL-RIS辅助的宽带信道估计方法，通过频率无关正交字典和压缩感知框架解决混合近远场和宽带效应带来的挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XL-RIS的大孔径导致用户设备可能工作在近场区域，而基站-XL-RIS链路仍处于远场，形成混合近远场特性的级联信道；同时毫米波/太赫兹系统中的宽带效应进一步增加了信道估计的复杂性

Method: 提出频率无关正交字典，通过增强DFT矩阵并添加额外参数，使宽带级联信道能够用二维块稀疏结构高效表示；基于此特性，在定制的压缩感知框架内有效解决信道估计问题

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在估计精度方面显著优于传统的极域信道估计方法

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了XL-RIS辅助OFDM系统中混合近远场和宽带效应带来的信道估计挑战，通过频率无关字典和压缩感知框架实现了更精确的信道估计

Abstract: This paper studies wideband channel estimation for OFDM systems assisted by extremely large RIS (XL-RIS). Due to the large aperture of XL-RISs, the user equipment may operate in the near-field region, while the base station-XL-RIS link remains in the far field, leading to a cascaded channel with hybrid near-field and far-field characteristics. Moreover, wideband effects further complicate channel estimation in mmWave/THz systems. To address these challenges, we propose a frequency-independent orthogonal dictionary by augmenting the discrete Fourier transform (DFT) matrix with additional parameters, which enables an efficient representation of the wideband cascaded channel using a two-dimensional block-sparse structure. Based on this property, the considered channel estimation problem is effectively solved within a tailored compressed sensing framework. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms conventional polar-domain channel estimation approaches in terms of estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [140] [APEG: Adaptive Physical Layer Authentication with Channel Extrapolation and Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21923)
*Xiqi Cheng,Rui Meng,Xiaodong Xu,Haixiao Gao,Ping Zhang,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出APEG框架，结合生成式AI和信道外推技术，通过CCMDM和CADM模型增强动态无线环境下的物理层身份认证鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G发展，身份认证对无线安全愈发重要。现有物理层认证方案难以适应动态无线环境，需要更鲁棒的认证方法

Method: 提出APEG框架，利用生成式AI自适应生成CSI指纹。包含CCMDM模型（利用协作方指纹作为条件输入进行信道外推）和CADM模型（采用交叉注意力机制对齐多尺度信道指纹特征）

Result: 仿真结果表明APEG框架在认证性能上优于现有基于时间序列的物理层认证方案。CCMDM在收敛速度上有显著优势，CADM相比无模型、时间序列和VAE方法在CSI指纹生成上达到更高准确率

Conclusion: APEG框架通过生成式AI和信道外推技术有效增强了动态无线环境下的物理层身份认证鲁棒性，为6G安全提供了有前景的解决方案

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of 6G, identity authentication has become increasingly critical for ensuring wireless security. The lightweight and keyless Physical Layer Authentication (PLA) is regarded as an instrumental security measure in addition to traditional cryptography-based authentication methods. However, existing PLA schemes often struggle to adapt to dynamic radio environments. To overcome this limitation, we propose the Adaptive PLA with Channel Extrapolation and Generative AI (APEG), designed to enhance authentication robustness in dynamic scenarios. Leveraging Generative AI (GAI), the framework adaptively generates Channel State Information (CSI) fingerprints, thereby improving the precision of identity verification. To refine CSI fingerprint generation, we propose the Collaborator-Cleaned Masked Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (CCMDM), which incorporates collaborator-provided fingerprints as conditional inputs for channel extrapolation. Additionally, we develop the Cross-Attention Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (CADM), employing a cross-attention mechanism to align multi-scale channel fingerprint features, further enhancing generation accuracy. Simulation results demonstrate the superiority of the APEG framework over existing time-sequence-based PLA schemes in authentication performance. Notably, CCMDM exhibits a significant advantage in convergence speed, while CADM, compared with model-free, time-series, and VAE-based methods, achieves superior accuracy in CSI fingerprint generation. The code is available at https://github.com/xiqicheng192-del/APEG

</details>


### [141] [Semi-Blind Channel Estimation and Hybrid Receiver Beamforming in the Tera-Hertz Multi-User Massive MIMO Uplink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22258)
*Abhisha Garg,Suraj Srivastava,Varsha Dubey,Aditya Jagannatham,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出针对太赫兹频段的多用户大规模MIMO信道模型，开发基于半盲的信道状态信息获取技术MU-WD-SB，设计基于MMV-SBL的混合接收机组合器框架，显著降低训练开销并提升信道学习精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹频段多用户大规模MIMO系统面临信道建模复杂、训练开销大、信道状态信息获取困难等挑战，需要开发高效的信道学习技术来降低训练开销并提高系统性能。

Method: 1) 开发包含分子吸收、反射损耗和多径扩散射线分量的太赫兹多用户大规模MIMO信道模型；2) 提出MU-WD-SB半盲信道状态信息获取技术，利用未知数据符号的二阶统计量和导频向量；3) 推导约束克拉美罗下界来界定NMSE性能；4) 设计基于MMV-SBL的混合接收机组合器框架，利用低分辨率ADC获取的估计CSI；5) 提出基于MMV-SBL的最优混合组合器来直接减少多用户干扰。

Result: 通过基于HITRAN数据库的实际太赫兹信道进行广泛仿真，评估了所提MU-WD-SB方案相对于传统训练方法和其他半盲学习技术的性能增益。在归一化均方误差、误码率和频谱效率等指标上均显示出显著改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的MU-WD-SB半盲学习技术能够有效降低训练开销，同时提高信道学习过程的整体精度。结合MMV-SBL的混合组合器框架进一步增强了系统性能，为太赫兹多用户大规模MIMO系统提供了实用的信道学习和干扰管理解决方案。

Abstract: We develop a pragmatic multi-user (MU) massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channel model tailored to the THz band, encompassing factors such as molecular absorption, reflection losses and multipath diffused ray components. Next, we propose a novel semi-blind based channel state information (CSI) acquisition technique i.e. MU whitening decorrelation semi-blind (MU-WD-SB) that exploits the second order statistics corresponding to the unknown data symbols along with pilot vectors. A constrained Cramer-Rao Lower Bound (C-CRLB) is derived to bound the normalized mean square error (NMSE) performance of the proposed semi-blind learning technique. Our proposed scheme efficiently reduces the training overheads while enhancing the overall accuracy of the channel learning process. Furthermore, a novel hybrid receiver combiner framework is devised for MU THz massive MIMO systems, leveraging multiple measurement vector based sparse Bayesian learning (MMV-SBL) that relies on the estimated CSI acquired through our proposed semi-blind technique relying on low resolution analog-to-digital converters (ADCs). Finally, we propose an optimal hybrid combiner based on MMV-SBL, which directly reduces the MU interference. Extensive simulations are conducted to evaluate the performance gain of the proposed MU-WD-SB scheme over conventional training-based and other semi-blind learning techniques for a practical THz channel obtained from the high-resolution transmission (HITRAN) database. The metrics considered for quantifying the improvements include the NMSE, bit error rate (BER) and spectral-efficiency (SE).

</details>
